+
+/// QEMU std VGA (PCI 1234:1111): Bochs VBE dispi registers, 1024x600x32 with the linear framebuffer at BAR0.
+fn q35_fb() -> Option<console::Fb> {
+    let b = pci::find(0x1234, 0x1111)?;
+    let lfb = pci::read32(b, 0x10) as u64 & !0xF;
+    let w = |i: u16, v: u16| unsafe {
+        port::outw(0x1CE, i);
+        port::outw(0x1CF, v);
+    };
+    w(4, 0); // disable while changing the mode
+    w(1, 1024);
+    w(2, 600);
+    w(3, 32);
+    w(6, 1024); // virtual width
+    w(4, 0x41); // enabled | linear framebuffer
+    Some(console::Fb { addr: lfb, pitch: 4096, w: 1024, h: 600, bytes: 4, rgb: [(16, 8), (8, 8), (0, 8)] })
+}

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Screenshots of the agent OS screens in q35 at the panel's 1024x600 (cmdline q35fb: Bochs VBE, not the GMA path), 1x.
+looks-q35-shots.py <outdir> [kernel]: boot.png (early boot), home.png, log.png (L), ssh.png (S: the SSH terminal; needs
+tools/docker-sshd/run.sh sshd_config.hold), then a second boot with cmdline recshow for rec.png (the REC overlay; QEMU
+has no ALC269 to record from). Exit 1 if a screen is missing or the framebuffer never came up."""
+import json, os, socket, subprocess, sys, time
+out = os.path.abspath(sys.argv[1]); os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
+K = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "target/attended/x86_64-unknown-none/release/nc110-kernel"
+fb_ok = []
+
+
+def run(cl, steps):
+    L, sock = f"/tmp/looks-q35-{os.getpid()}.log", f"/tmp/looks-q35-{os.getpid()}.qmp"
+    for p in (L, sock):
+        if os.path.exists(p): os.unlink(p)
+    q = subprocess.Popen(["qemu-system-x86_64", "-machine", "q35", "-m", "128", "-display", "none", "-no-reboot",
+        "-global", "ICH9-LPC.noreboot=false", "-serial", f"file:{L}", "-qmp", f"unix:{sock},server=on,wait=off",
+        "-kernel", K, "-initrd", "test-keys/client.key", "-append", cl,
+        "-netdev", "user,id=n0", "-device", "rtl8139,netdev=n0"])
+    log = lambda: open(L, errors="replace").read() if os.path.exists(L) else ""
+    for _ in range(50):
+        if os.path.exists(sock): break
+        time.sleep(0.05)
+    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX); s.connect(sock); f = s.makefile("rw"); f.readline()
+    def qmp(c):
+        f.write(json.dumps(c) + "\n"); f.flush()
+        while True:
+            r = json.loads(f.readline())
+            if "return" in r or "error" in r: return r
+    qmp({"execute": "qmp_capabilities"})
+    shot = lambda n: qmp({"execute": "screendump", "arguments": {"filename": f"{out}/{n}.png", "format": "png"}})
+    key = lambda k: (qmp({"execute": "send-key", "arguments": {"keys": [{"type": "qcode", "data": k}]}}), time.sleep(0.8))
+    def wait(p):
+        for _ in range(160):
+            if p in log(): return
+            time.sleep(0.05 if p == "q35fb:" else 0.25)
+    steps(shot, key, wait)
+    q.kill(); s.close()
+    fb_ok.append("q35fb: Bochs VBE 1024x600x32: true" in log())
+
+
+def main_run(shot, key, wait):
+    wait("q35fb:"); time.sleep(0.2); shot("boot")
+    wait("home: menu up"); time.sleep(1); shot("home")
+    key("l"); time.sleep(0.5); shot("log"); key("l")
+    key("s"); time.sleep(1.5); shot("ssh")
+
+
+def rec_run(shot, key, wait):
+    wait("home: menu up"); time.sleep(1); shot("rec")
+
+
+run("bmctl nic=mmio q35fb", main_run)
+run("bmctl nic=mmio q35fb recshow", rec_run)
+ok = all(fb_ok) and all(os.path.exists(f"{out}/{n}.png") for n in ("boot", "home", "log", "ssh", "rec"))
+print("RESULT:", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL"); sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)

     1	//! Output to COM1 (0x3F8, 115200 8N1) and an 80x25 cell screen: the VGA text buffer (0xB8000), or, when GRUB set a
     2	//! linear framebuffer (multiboot video mode, the panel's native 1024x600), the same cells drawn at native resolution
     3	//! with the 12x24 font (960x600, centred). Every cell write goes through `cell`, which keeps a shadow copy and only
     4	//! redraws cells that changed.
     5	//! The screen shows a quiet splash (title, one status line, a footer bar) by default; the log still goes to COM1,
     6	//! the RAM capture (bmctl `log`, udplog) and comes back on screen with `show_log` (cmdline `verbose`, panics).
     7	use crate::port::{inb, outb};
     8	use core::fmt;
     9	
    10	const COM1: u16 = 0x3F8;
    11	const VGA: *mut u16 = 0xB8000 as *mut u16;
    12	const MAXW: usize = crate::term::MAXW;
    13	const MAXH: usize = crate::term::MAXH;
    14	static mut COLS: usize = 80; // VGA text: 80x25; framebuffer: the panel divided by the font cell
    15	static mut ROWS: usize = 25;
    16	static mut FONT: &crate::font::Font = &crate::font::F12X24;
    17	
    18	pub fn is_fb() -> bool {
    19	    unsafe { (*core::ptr::addr_of!(FB)).is_some() }
    20	}
    21	
    22	pub fn cols() -> usize {
    23	    unsafe { COLS }
    24	}
    25	pub fn rows() -> usize {
    26	    unsafe { ROWS }
    27	}
    28	fn font() -> &'static crate::font::Font {
    29	    unsafe { FONT }
    30	}
    31	const ATTR: u16 = 0x0700; // the log: light grey on the background (was white on blue)

    33	/// Linear framebuffer from multiboot: address, pitch, width, height, bytes per pixel, (r, g, b) (position, size).
    34	#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
    35	pub struct Fb {
    36	    pub addr: u64,
    37	    pub pitch: u32,
    38	    pub w: u32,
    39	    pub h: u32,
    40	    pub bytes: u32,
    41	    pub rgb: [(u8, u8); 3],
    42	}
    43	
    44	static mut FB: Option<(Fb, u32, u32)> = None; // framebuffer, x0, y0 of the 960x600 cell area
    45	static mut SHADOW: [u16; MAXW * MAXH] = [0; MAXW * MAXH];
    46	static mut CURSOR: Option<usize> = None;
    47	static mut POINTER: Option<usize> = None;
    48	
    49	/// The mouse pointer cell is shown with its attribute XORed with 0x77 (fg/bg swapped-ish, bright bits kept).
    50	fn shown(i: usize, v: u16) -> u16 {
    51	    if let Some(c) = corner_cell(i) {
    52	        c
    53	    } else if unsafe { POINTER } == Some(i) {
    54	        v ^ 0x7700
    55	    } else {
    56	        v
    57	    }
    58	}
    59	
    60	/// Move the mouse pointer to cell `pos` (None hides it). The shadow keeps the real cells.
    61	pub fn pointer(pos: Option<usize>) {
    62	    unsafe {
    63	        let old = POINTER;
    64	        if old == pos {
    65	            return;
    66	        }
    67	        POINTER = pos;
    68	        for i in [old, pos].into_iter().flatten() {
    69	            let v = shown(i, cell_get(i));
    70	            if (*core::ptr::addr_of!(FB)).is_some() {
    71	                draw(i, v, CURSOR == Some(i));
    72	            } else {
    73	                VGA.add(i).write_volatile(v);
    74	            }
    75	        }
    76	    }
    77	}
    78	
    79	// Attribute colours 0..15 as RGB on the framebuffer: a dark theme with tested contrast (GitHub dark's palette), used by
    80	// the home screen and the SSH terminal alike. 0 is the background (#0D1117, not pure black); 7 (the terminal's default
    81	// text) is 12:1 on it, 8 (secondary text) 6:1. VGA text mode keeps the hardware palette.
    82	const PAL: [u32; 16] = [0x0D1117, 0x58A6FF, 0x3FB950, 0x39C5CF, 0xF85149, 0xBC8CFF, 0xD29922, 0xC9D1D9, 0x8B949E, 0x79C0FF,
    83	    0x56D364, 0x56D4DD, 0xFF7B72, 0xD2A8FF, 0xE3B341, 0xF0F6FC];
    84	
    85	fn pixel(fb: &Fb, rgb: u32) -> u32 {
    86	    let ch = |v: u32, (pos, size): (u8, u8)| (v >> (8 - size.min(8))) << (pos & 31);
    87	    ch(rgb >> 16 & 0xFF, fb.rgb[0]) | ch(rgb >> 8 & 0xFF, fb.rgb[1]) | ch(rgb & 0xFF, fb.rgb[2])
    88	}
    89	
    90	fn draw(i: usize, v: u16, underline: bool) {
    91	    let Some((fb, x0, y0)) = (unsafe { *core::ptr::addr_of!(FB) }) else { return };
    92	    let attr = (v >> 8) as usize;
    93	    let (fg, bg) = (pixel(&fb, PAL[attr & 0xF]), pixel(&fb, PAL[attr >> 4 & 0xF]));
    94	    let f = font();
    95	    let g = f.glyph((v & 0xFF) as u8);
    96	    let (cx, cy) = (x0 + (i % cols()) as u32 * f.w as u32, y0 + (i / cols()) as u32 * f.h as u32);
    97	    let big = title_rect().filter(|&(r0, c0, w, h)| unsafe { TITLE_ON }
    98	        && (r0..r0 + h).contains(&(i / cols())) && (c0..c0 + w).contains(&(i % cols())) && cell_get(i) >> 8 == TITLE >> 8);
    99	    // The side margins (1024 - 960 = 2 x 32 px on the NC110) take the background of the edge cell next to them, so
   100	    // the footer bar and coloured terminal rows reach the panel edges.
   101	    let margin = match i % cols() {
   102	        0 => Some((0, x0)),
   103	        c if c == cols() - 1 => Some((cx + f.w as u32, fb.w)),
   104	        _ => None,
   105	    };
   106	    if let Some((mx0, mx1)) = margin {
   107	        for y in 0..f.h as u32 {
   108	            let line = fb.addr + (cy + y) as u64 * fb.pitch as u64;
   109	            for x in mx0..mx1 {
   110	                let a = line + (x * fb.bytes) as u64;
   111	                unsafe {
   112	                    match fb.bytes {
   113	                        4 => core::ptr::write_volatile(a as *mut u32, bg),
   114	                        3 => {
   115	                            core::ptr::write_volatile(a as *mut u16, bg as u16);
   116	                            core::ptr::write_volatile((a + 2) as *mut u8, (bg >> 16) as u8);
   117	                        }
   118	                        _ => core::ptr::write_volatile(a as *mut u16, bg as u16),
   119	                    }
   120	                }
   121	            }
   122	        }
   123	    }
   124	    for (y, &bits) in g.iter().enumerate() {
   125	        let bits = if underline && y + 3 >= f.h && y + 1 < f.h { (1 << f.w) - 1 } else { bits };
   126	        // The title: this cell's slice of TITLE_TEXT drawn TITLE_SCALE times the font size.
   127	        let bits = match big {
   128	            Some((r0, c0, _, _)) => {
   129	                let py = ((i / cols() - r0) * f.h + y) / TITLE_SCALE;
   130	                (0..f.w).fold(0u16, |acc, x| {
   131	                    let px = ((i % cols() - c0) * f.w + x) / TITLE_SCALE;
   132	                    let on = f.glyph(TITLE_TEXT[px / f.w])[py] >> (f.w - 1 - px % f.w) & 1;
   133	                    acc | on << (f.w - 1 - x)
   134	                })
   135	            }
   136	            None => bits,
   137	        };
   138	        let line = fb.addr + (cy + y as u32) as u64 * fb.pitch as u64 + cx as u64 * fb.bytes as u64;
   139	        for x in 0..f.w {
   140	            let p = if bits & (1 << (f.w - 1 - x)) != 0 { fg } else { bg };
   141	            let a = line + (x as u32 * fb.bytes) as u64;
   142	            unsafe {
   143	                match fb.bytes {
   144	                    4 => core::ptr::write_volatile(a as *mut u32, p),
   145	                    3 => {
   146	                        core::ptr::write_volatile(a as *mut u16, p as u16);
   147	                        core::ptr::write_volatile((a + 2) as *mut u8, (p >> 16) as u8);
   148	                    }
   149	                    _ => core::ptr::write_volatile(a as *mut u16, p as u16),
   150	                }
   151	            }
   152	        }
   153	    }
   154	}
   155	
   156	/// Set one cell (VGA attribute << 8 | CP437 char). Only a changed cell is redrawn.
   157	pub fn cell(i: usize, v: u16) {
   158	    unsafe {
   159	        let sh = &mut *core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(SHADOW);
   160	        if sh[i] == v {
   161	            return;
   162	        }
   163	        sh[i] = v;
   164	        if v >> 8 != TITLE >> 8 && title_rect().is_some_and(|(r0, c0, w, h)| (r0..r0 + h).contains(&(i / cols())) && (c0..c0 + w).contains(&(i % cols()))) {
   165	            TITLE_ON = false; // something else (the terminal) owns this area now
   166	        }
   167	        let v = shown(i, v);
   168	        if (*core::ptr::addr_of!(FB)).is_some() {
   169	            draw(i, v, CURSOR == Some(i));
   170	        } else {
   171	            VGA.add(i).write_volatile(v);
   172	        }
   173	    }
   174	}
   175	
   176	/// Redraw cell `i` from the shadow (framebuffer only).
   177	fn draw_now(i: usize) {
   178	    if is_fb() {
   179	        draw(i, shown(i, cell_get(i)), unsafe { CURSOR } == Some(i));
   180	    }
   181	}
   182	
   183	pub fn cell_get(i: usize) -> u16 {
   184	    unsafe { (*core::ptr::addr_of!(SHADOW))[i] }
   185	}
   186	
   187	/// Text cursor at cell `pos` (None hides it): the VGA hardware cursor, or an underline on the framebuffer.
   188	pub fn cursor(pos: Option<usize>) {
   189	    unsafe {
   190	        if (*core::ptr::addr_of!(FB)).is_some() {
   191	            let old = CURSOR;
   192	            CURSOR = pos;
   193	            if old != pos {
   194	                if let Some(o) = old {
   195	                    draw(o, shown(o, cell_get(o)), false);
   196	                }
   197	                if let Some(p) = pos {
   198	                    draw(p, shown(p, cell_get(p)), true);
   199	                }
   200	            }
   201	            return;
   202	        }
   203	        match pos {
   204	            None => {
   205	                outb(0x3D4, 0x0A);
   206	                outb(0x3D5, 0x20); // bit 5 hides the cursor
   207	            }
   208	            Some(p) => {
   209	                outb(0x3D4, 0x0A);
   210	                outb(0x3D5, 13); // scanlines 13..14
   211	                outb(0x3D4, 0x0B);
   212	                outb(0x3D5, 14);
   213	                outb(0x3D4, 0x0F);
   214	                outb(0x3D5, p as u8);
   215	                outb(0x3D4, 0x0E);
   216	                outb(0x3D5, (p >> 8) as u8);
   217	            }
   218	        }
   219	    }
   220	}
   221	
   222	/// Switch the screen to a multiboot linear framebuffer (needs >= 960x600 and 16/24/32 bpp): clear it and redraw every
   223	/// cell from the shadow. False = not usable (the caller keeps VGA text, which a graphics mode will not show).
   224	pub fn use_framebuffer(fb: Fb) -> bool {
   225	    let f = font();
   226	    if fb.w < 80 * f.w as u32 || fb.h < 25 * f.h as u32 || !(2..=4).contains(&fb.bytes) || fb.pitch < fb.w * fb.bytes {
   227	        return false;
   228	    }
   229	    if !crate::nic::map_fb(fb.addr, fb.pitch as u64 * fb.h as u64) {
   230	        return false;
   231	    }
   232	    unsafe {
   233	        FB = Some((fb, 0, 0));
   234	    }
   235	    set_font(f);
   236	    true
   237	}
   238	
   239	/// Framebuffer only: switch the cell font (the grid becomes the panel divided by the cell, up to 170x50) and repaint
   240	/// from the shadow. The callers redraw their screen (splash / terminal) at the new size.
   241	pub fn set_font(f: &'static crate::font::Font) {
   242	    unsafe {
   243	        let Some((fb, _, _)) = *core::ptr::addr_of!(FB) else { return };
   244	        let (c, r) = ((fb.w as usize / f.w).min(MAXW), (fb.h as usize / f.h).min(MAXH));
   245	        FONT = f;
   246	        (COLS, ROWS) = (c, r);
   247	        FB = Some((fb, (fb.w - (c * f.w) as u32) / 2, (fb.h - (r * f.h) as u32) / 2));
   248	        CURSOR = None;
   249	        POINTER = None;
   250	        let bg = pixel(&fb, PAL[0]);
   251	        for y in 0..fb.h as u64 {
   252	            for x in 0..fb.w as u64 {
   253	                let a = fb.addr + y * fb.pitch as u64 + x * fb.bytes as u64;
   254	                match fb.bytes {
   255	                    4 => core::ptr::write_volatile(a as *mut u32, bg),
   256	                    3 => {
   257	                        core::ptr::write_volatile(a as *mut u16, bg as u16);
   258	                        core::ptr::write_volatile((a + 2) as *mut u8, (bg >> 16) as u8);
   259	                    }
   260	                    _ => core::ptr::write_volatile(a as *mut u16, bg as u16),
   261	                }
   262	            }
   263	        }
   264	        (*core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(SHADOW)).fill(0);
   265	    }
   266	}
   267	
   268	/// The framebuffer fonts, smallest cell last: (name, font).
   269	pub const FONTS: [(&str, &crate::font::Font); 3] =
   270	    [("12x24", &crate::font::F12X24), ("8x16", &crate::font::F8X16), ("6x12", &crate::font::F6X12)];
   271	
   272	/// Cycle to the next font (framebuffer only). Returns the new grid, or None on VGA text.
   273	pub fn next_font() -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
   274	    unsafe {
   275	        (*core::ptr::addr_of!(FB))?;
   276	        let cur = FONTS.iter().position(|(_, f)| core::ptr::eq(*f, FONT)).unwrap_or(0);
   277	        set_font(FONTS[(cur + 1) % FONTS.len()].1);
   278	        Some((COLS, ROWS))
   279	    }
   280	}
   281	
   282	static mut ROW: usize = 0;
   283	static mut COL: usize = 0;
   284	/// Log lines also go to the screen (verbose / panic). Off for the splash and the SSH terminal.
   285	static mut VGA_LOG: bool = false;
   286	
   287	pub fn vga_log_off() {
   288	    unsafe { VGA_LOG = false };
   289	}
   290	
   291	/// Blank the screen in the log colours, replay the last lines of the capture, then keep logging to it.
   292	pub fn show_log() {
   293	    unsafe {
   294	        fill(ATTR);
   295	        ROW = 0;
   296	        COL = 0;
   297	        let c = captured();
   298	        let mut st = c.len();
   299	        for _ in 0..rows() - 1 {
   300	            match c[..st.saturating_sub(1)].iter().rposition(|&b| b == b'\n') {
   301	                Some(i) => st = i + 1,
   302	                None => {
   303	                    st = 0;
   304	                    break;
   305	                }
   306	            }
   307	        }
   308	        for &b in &c[st..] {
   309	            vga_putc(b);
   310	        }
   311	        VGA_LOG = true;
   312	    }
   313	}
   314	
   315	fn fill(attr: u16) {
   316	    unsafe { TITLE_ON = false };
   317	    for i in 0..cols() * rows() {
   318	        cell(i, attr | b' ' as u16);
   319	    }
   320	}
   321	
   322	fn put(row: usize, col: usize, attr: u16, s: &[u8]) {
   323	    for (i, &b) in s.iter().enumerate().take(cols().saturating_sub(col)) {
   324	        cell(row * cols() + col + i, attr | b as u16);
   325	    }
   326	}
   327	
   328	fn put_center(row: usize, attr: u16, s: &[u8], bg: u16) {
   329	    put(row, 0, bg, &[b' '; MAXW][..cols()]);
   330	    put(row, (cols() - s.len().min(cols())) / 2, attr, s);
   331	}
   332	
   333	const BG: u16 = 0x0000; // background
   334	const TITLE: u16 = 0x0F00; // near-white (the title area's marker attribute too)
   335	/// Secondary text: palette 8 on the framebuffer (6.2:1); light grey in VGA text mode, whose dark grey is only 2.8:1.
   336	fn dim() -> u16 {
   337	    if is_fb() { 0x0800 } else { 0x0700 }
   338	}
   339	const TEXT: u16 = 0x0F00; // primary text
   340	const KEY: u16 = 0x0900; // accent: keys
   341	const OK: u16 = 0x0A00; // green dot: ready
   342	const REC_DOT: u16 = 0x0C00; // red dot: recording
   343	const BAR: u16 = 0x7000; // dark on light grey: the selected chooser row
   344	const LINE: u16 = 0x0800; // the footer's separator line
   345	/// Splash rows: the 25-row layout, centred vertically on taller grids.
   346	fn splash_row() -> usize {
   347	    rows().saturating_sub(25) / 2 + 6
   348	}
   349	fn status_row() -> usize {
   350	    splash_row() + 7
   351	}
   352	
   353	/// The title, drawn TITLE_SCALE x the cell font over a block of cells (framebuffer); plain text in VGA text mode.
   354	const TITLE_TEXT: &[u8] = b"NC110";
   355	const TITLE_SCALE: usize = 3;
   356	static mut TITLE_ON: bool = false;
   357	/// (row, col, cells wide, cells high) of the title.
   358	fn title_rect() -> Option<(usize, usize, usize, usize)> {
   359	    let (w, h) = if is_fb() { (TITLE_TEXT.len() * TITLE_SCALE, TITLE_SCALE) } else { (TITLE_TEXT.len(), 1) };
   360	    Some((splash_row() + if is_fb() { 0 } else { 2 }, cols().checked_sub(w)? / 2, w, h))
   361	}
   362	
   363	/// The quiet boot screen: logo, subtitle, empty status line and footer. Log lines stop reaching the screen.
   364	pub fn splash() {
   365	    unsafe {
   366	        VGA_LOG = false;
   367	        CHOOSER = None;
   368	    }
   369	    cursor(None);
   370	    fill(BG);
   371	    if let Some((r0, c0, w, h)) = title_rect() {
   372	        for r in r0..r0 + h {
   373	            put(r, c0, TITLE, &[b' '; MAXW][..w]);
   374	        }
   375	        put(r0, c0, TITLE, TITLE_TEXT); // the text itself in the first row: VGA shows it, bmctl `screen` reads it
   376	        unsafe { TITLE_ON = true };
   377	        for r in r0..r0 + h {
   378	            for c in c0..c0 + w {
   379	                draw_now(r * cols() + c);
   380	            }
   381	        }
   382	    }
   383	    put_center(splash_row() + 4, dim(), b"agentic OS", BG);
   384	    footer(b"", b"");
   385	}
   386	
   387	/// One centred status line under the logo (ignored while the log or the SSH terminal owns the screen).
   388	pub fn status(s: &str) {
   389	    if unsafe { VGA_LOG } || !splash_shown() {
   390	        return;
   391	    }
   392	    put_center(status_row(), TEXT, s.as_bytes(), BG);
   393	    let x = (cols() - s.len().min(cols())) / 2;
   394	    if x >= 2 {
   395	        // A dot: green when ready, red while recording (the corner REC stays for the other screens).
   396	        put(status_row(), x - 2, if s == "ready" { OK } else if s.starts_with("recording") { REC_DOT } else { dim() }, &[0x07]);
   397	    }
   398	}
   399	
   400	static mut MENU_HITS: [(usize, usize, u8); 8] = [(0, 0, 0); 8];
   401	static mut CHOOSER: Option<(usize, usize)> = None; // first row, rows: the host chooser's click targets
   402	
   403	/// Host chooser rows under the logo (replacing the status line and the key menu); `sel` highlighted.
   404	pub fn chooser(items: &[&str], sel: usize) {
   405	    if unsafe { VGA_LOG } || !splash_shown() {
   406	        return;
   407	    }
   408	    let first = status_row();
   409	    for r in status_row() - 1..rows() - 2 {
   410	        put(r, 0, BG, &[b' '; MAXW][..cols()]);
   411	    }
   412	    put_center(status_row() - 1, dim(), b"SSH: choose a host", BG);
   413	    let w = items.iter().map(|s| s.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
   414	    let x = cols().saturating_sub(w) / 2;
   415	    for (i, it) in items.iter().enumerate() {
   416	        put(first + i, x, if i == sel { BAR } else { TEXT }, it.as_bytes());
   417	    }
   418	    put_center(rows() - 3, dim(), b"1-8 or click: connect    arrows + Enter    Esc: back", BG);
   419	    unsafe {
   420	        *core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MENU_HITS) = [(0, 0, 0); 8];
   421	        CHOOSER = Some((first, items.len()));
   422	    }
   423	}
   424	
   425	/// The menu key under a click at cell (col, row) on the splash, if any.
   426	pub fn menu_hit(col: usize, row: usize) -> Option<u8> {
   427	    if unsafe { VGA_LOG } || !splash_shown() {
   428	        return None;
   429	    }
   430	    if let Some((first, n)) = unsafe { CHOOSER } {
   431	        return (first..first + n).contains(&row).then(|| b'1' + (row - first) as u8);
   432	    }
   433	    if row != status_row() + 4 {
   434	        return None;
   435	    }
   436	    unsafe { (*core::ptr::addr_of!(MENU_HITS)).iter().find(|&&(a, b, k)| k != 0 && (a..b).contains(&col)).map(|h| h.2) }
   437	}
   438	
   439	/// Key hints under the status line: each key as a light chip, then its label.
   440	pub fn menu(items: &[(&str, &str)]) {
   441	    if unsafe { VGA_LOG } || !splash_shown() {
   442	        return;
   443	    }
   444	    let row = status_row() + 4;
   445	    let w: usize = items.iter().map(|(k, l)| k.len() + 2 + 1 + l.len()).sum::<usize>() + 4 * items.len().saturating_sub(1);
   446	    put(row, 0, BG, &[b' '; MAXW][..cols()]);
   447	    let mut x = cols().saturating_sub(w) / 2;
   448	    let hits = unsafe { &mut *core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MENU_HITS) };
   449	    *hits = [(0, 0, 0); 8];
   450	    for (n, (k, l)) in items.iter().enumerate() {
   451	        if n < 8 {
   452	            hits[n] = (x, x + k.len() + 3 + l.len(), k.as_bytes()[0]);
   453	        }
   454	        put(row, x + 1, KEY, k.as_bytes());
   455	        x += k.len() + 2;
   456	        put(row, x + 1, TEXT, l.as_bytes());
   457	        x += 1 + l.len() + 4;
   458	    }
   459	}
   460	
   461	static mut CORNER: Option<[u8; 5]> = None; // overlay text in the top-right corner (REC), drawn over any screen
   462	
   463	fn corner_cell(i: usize) -> Option<u16> {
   464	    let c = unsafe { (*core::ptr::addr_of!(CORNER))? };
   465	    let at = cols().checked_sub(5)?;
   466	    (at..at + 5).contains(&i).then(|| 0x4000 | c[i - at] as u16) // background-dark text on red: 5.6:1 (white was 3.1:1)
   467	}
   468	
   469	/// A red marker in the top-right corner, an overlay like the pointer: the SSH terminal, a font change or the splash
   470	/// cannot erase it while it is set (Codex review). None removes it and shows the real cells again.
   471	pub fn corner(s: Option<&[u8]>) {
   472	    unsafe {
   473	        let mut c = [b' '; 5];
   474	        if let Some(t) = s {
   475	            for (d, &b) in c[1..].iter_mut().zip(t) {
   476	                *d = b;
   477	            }
   478	        }
   479	        CORNER = s.map(|_| c);
   480	        let at = cols() - 5;
   481	        for i in at..at + 5 {
   482	            let v = shown(i, cell_get(i));
   483	            if (*core::ptr::addr_of!(FB)).is_some() {
   484	                draw(i, v, CURSOR == Some(i));
   485	            } else {
   486	                VGA.add(i).write_volatile(v);
   487	            }
   488	        }
   489	    }
   490	}
   491	
   492	/// Footer bar: `left` at the left edge, `right` at the right edge.
   493	pub fn footer(left: &[u8], right: &[u8]) {
   494	    if unsafe { VGA_LOG } || !splash_shown() {
   495	        return;
   496	    }
   497	    put(rows() - 2, 0, BG, &[b' '; MAXW][..cols()]);
   498	    put(rows() - 2, 2, LINE, &[0xC4; MAXW][..cols().saturating_sub(4)]); // inset to the text margins
   499	    put(rows() - 1, 0, BG, &[b' '; MAXW][..cols()]);
   500	    put(rows() - 1, 2, dim(), left);
   501	    let x = cols().saturating_sub(right.len() + 2);
   502	    put(rows() - 1, x, dim(), right);
   503	    if let Some(k) = right.iter().rposition(|&b| b == b' ') {
   504	        put(rows() - 1, x + k + 1, TEXT, &right[k + 1..]); // the clock (last field) stands out
   505	    }
   506	}
   507	
   508	/// The splash owns the screen iff its logo is there (the SSH terminal or the log may have drawn over it).
   509	fn splash_shown() -> bool {
   510	    title_rect().is_some_and(|(r0, c0, _, _)| cell_get(r0 * cols() + c0) == (TITLE | b'N' as u16))
   511	}
   512	
   513	/// Raw byte to COM1 (used to mirror the remote shell's output into the serial log).
   514	pub fn serial_raw(b: u8) {
   515	    serial_putc(b);
   516	}
   517	
   518	pub fn init() {
   519	    unsafe {
   520	        outb(COM1 + 1, 0x00); // no interrupts
   521	        outb(COM1 + 3, 0x80); // DLAB
   522	        outb(COM1 + 0, 0x01); // divisor 1 = 115200
   523	        outb(COM1 + 1, 0x00);
   524	        outb(COM1 + 3, 0x03); // 8N1
   525	        outb(COM1 + 2, 0xC7); // FIFO
   526	        outb(COM1 + 4, 0x03);
   527	    }
   528	    splash();
   529	}
   530	
   531	fn serial_putc(b: u8) {
   532	    unsafe {
   533	        // Bounded: a wedged or absent UART must never keep a failure path from reaching its 0xCF9 reset.
   534	        for _ in 0..100_000 {
   535	            if inb(COM1 + 5) & 0x20 != 0 {
   536	                outb(COM1, b);
   537	                return;
   538	            }
   539	        }
   540	    }
   541	}
   542	
   543	fn vga_putc(b: u8) {
   544	    unsafe {
   545	        if b == b'\n' {
   546	            COL = 0;
   547	            ROW += 1;
   548	        } else {
   549	            cell(ROW * cols() + COL, ATTR | b as u16);
   550	            COL += 1;
   551	            if COL == cols() {
   552	                COL = 0;
   553	                ROW += 1;
   554	            }
   555	        }
   556	        if ROW == rows() {
   557	            for i in 0..cols() * (rows() - 1) {
   558	                cell(i, cell_get(i + cols()));
   559	            }
   560	            for i in 0..cols() {
   561	                cell((rows() - 1) * cols() + i, ATTR | b' ' as u16);
   562	            }
   563	            ROW = rows() - 1;
   564	        }
   565	    }
   566	}
   567	
   568	/// RAM copy of everything printed (first 8 KB), so the attended build can ship its log over UDP.
   569	static mut CAP: [u8; 8192] = [0; 8192];
   570	static mut CAP_LEN: usize = 0;
   571	
   572	pub fn captured() -> &'static [u8] {
   573	    unsafe { core::slice::from_raw_parts(core::ptr::addr_of!(CAP) as *const u8, CAP_LEN) }
   574	}
   575	
   576	static mut AT_LINE_START: bool = true;
   577	
   578	pub struct Console;
   579	impl fmt::Write for Console {
   580	    fn write_str(&mut self, s: &str) -> fmt::Result {
   581	        if unsafe { AT_LINE_START } && crate::time::ready() && !s.is_empty() && !s.starts_with(['\r', '\n']) {
   582	            unsafe { AT_LINE_START = false };
   583	            let t = crate::time::now_ms();
   584	            write!(self, "[{:3}.{:03}] ", t / 1000, t % 1000)?;
   585	        }
   586	        for &b in s.as_bytes() {
   587	            if b == b'\n' {
   588	                serial_putc(b'\r');
   589	            }
   590	            serial_putc(b);
   591	            unsafe {
   592	                if CAP_LEN < 8192 {
   593	                    (*core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(CAP))[CAP_LEN] = b;
   594	                    CAP_LEN += 1;
   595	                }
   596	            }
   597	            if unsafe { VGA_LOG } {
   598	                vga_putc(b);
   599	            }
   600	            if b == b'\n' {
   601	                unsafe { AT_LINE_START = true };
   602	            }
   603	        }
   604	        Ok(())
   605	    }
   606	}
   607	
   608	#[macro_export]
   609	macro_rules! kprintln {
   610	    ($($arg:tt)*) => {{
   611	        use core::fmt::Write;
   612	        let _ = writeln!($crate::console::Console, $($arg)*);
   613	    }};
   614	}
   615	
   616	#[macro_export]
   617	macro_rules! kprint {
   618	    ($($arg:tt)*) => {{
   619	        use core::fmt::Write;
   620	        let _ = write!($crate::console::Console, $($arg)*);
   621	    }};
   622	}
   623	
   624	/// The text of screen row `r` as the user sees it (shadow + REC overlay; pointer not marked): bmctl `screen`.
   625	pub fn row_text(r: usize, out: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
   626	    if r >= rows() {
   627	        return 0;
   628	    }
   629	    let n = cols().min(out.len());
   630	    for (c, b) in out[..n].iter_mut().enumerate() {
   631	        let i = r * cols() + c;
   632	        let v = corner_cell(i).unwrap_or_else(|| cell_get(i));
   633	        *b = match (v & 0xFF) as u8 {
   634	            0xDB => b'#', // the logo's full blocks
   635	            x @ 0x20..=0x7E => x,
   636	            0 => b' ',
   637	            _ => b'?',
   638	        };
   639	    }
   640	    n
   641	}

//! Generated by tools/font/gen-font.py: Spleen (c) 2018-2024 Frederic Cambus, BSD 2-Clause (tools/font/LICENSE.spleen),
//! as CP437. Each glyph row holds the pixels in its low `w` bits, leftmost = bit w-1.
pub struct Font {
    pub w: usize,
    pub h: usize,
    pub rows: &'static [u16],
}
impl Font {
    pub fn glyph(&self, c: u8) -> &[u16] {
        &self.rows[c as usize * self.h..(c as usize + 1) * self.h]
    }
}

[package]
name = "nc110-kernel"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"

[features]
neg-control = []
grub-armed = []
attended = []  # NC110 with evnchn at the machine: no watchdog gate (the TCO hangs this board instead of resetting it), 0xCF9 reset after 60 s
badpin = []  # negative control: flip one bit of the pinned host key
badjitter = []  # negative control: constant TSC in the jitter source, startup health test must refuse SSH

[dependencies]
# M2: pinned exact versions (Cargo.lock is committed too).
sunset = { version = "=0.6.0", default-features = false }
smoltcp = { version = "=0.14.0", default-features = false, features = ["medium-ethernet", "proto-ipv4", "socket-tcp", "socket-dhcpv4", "socket-udp"] }

log = "0.4"
heapless = { version = "=0.9.3", default-features = false }  # M3: sunset::Pty.term is a heapless::String
sha2 = { version = "=0.11.1", default-features = false }
chacha20 = { version = "=0.9.1", default-features = false }  # bmctl AEAD (RFC 8439), same versions sunset already pulls in
poly1305 = { version = "=0.9.1", default-features = false }

    kprintln!("time: TSC {} ticks/ms (PIT ch2 calibrated); {} ms since CPU reset", time::init(), time::since_reset_ms());
    // GRUB t7 passes the TSC at its entry start and just before `multiboot`: split firmware / GRUB / our early boot.
    if let Some((g0, g1)) = cmdline.split(' ').find_map(|w| w.strip_prefix("grubtsc=")).and_then(|v| {

    }) {

        kprintln!("boot: firmware + GRUB core {} ms, GRUB entry {} ms, multiboot load + our start {} ms", a, b.saturating_sub(a), k.saturating_sub(b));
    }

    // Mic capture is on by default in the attended agent OS (`nohda` turns it off); `hdadump` also logs every widget.
    if cfg!(feature = "attended") && !cmdline.split(' ').any(|w| w == "nohda") {
        hda::probe(cmdline.split(' ').any(|w| w == "hdadump"));
    }
    if cmdline.split(' ').any(|w| w == "gmadump") {
        gma::dump();
    }
    // Native 1024x600 is the agent OS default (evnchn card 850); `nogma` keeps the VBIOS text mode. native_fb falls back
    // to the VBIOS VGA mode by itself when its checks fail (no LVDS 1024x600 on pipe B, FIFO underrun after the switch).
    let native = cfg!(feature = "attended") && !cmdline.split(' ').any(|w| w == "nogma") || cmdline.split(' ').any(|w| w == "gmafb");
    if !native && cfg!(feature = "attended") {
        gma::restore_vga_if_needed();
    }
    if cmdline.split(' ').any(|w| w == "q35fb") {
        // Emulator only: QEMU's std VGA (Bochs VBE) at the panel's 1024x600, so screen work can be checked pixel-exact in
        // q35 without the NC110's GMA path (gma.rs is never touched by this).
        if let Some(fb) = q35_fb() {
            kprintln!("q35fb: Bochs VBE 1024x600x32: {}", console::use_framebuffer(fb));
        }
        if cmdline.split(' ').any(|w| w == "recshow") {

        }
    } else if native {
        if let Some(fb) = gma::native_fb() {

            kprintln!("gma: console on the native framebuffer: {}", ok);

                gma::undo_native();
            }
        }

    if let Some(f) = cmdline.split(' ').find_map(|w| w.strip_prefix("font=")) {
        if let Some((_, font)) = console::FONTS.iter().find(|(n, _)| *n == f) {
            console::set_font(font);
        }
    }
    {
        console::splash(); // the framebuffer (if any) starts blank: draw the boot screen at its grid
        console::status("starting");
        kprintln!("console: {}x{} cells", console::cols(), console::rows());
    }
    if cmdline.split(' ').any(|w| w == "phyreset") {
        nic::force_phy_reset();
    }
    let mut no_net = false; // no Ethernet link: restart fast, so the day's 6th boot (recovery Linux, WiFi) comes soon
    match nic::probe(cmdline.contains("nic=mmio")) {
        Ok(mut n) => {
            if cmdline.contains("nic=no8105e") {
                n.skip_8105e(); // A/B switch for the rtl_hw_start_8105e tweaks
            }
            kprintln!("nic: {} at {} {} mac={}", n.name(), n.bdf, n.bus, dhcp::Mac(n.mac));
            match n.init() {
                Ok(()) if cfg!(feature = "attended") => {
                    kprintln!("udplog: broadcasting this log to UDP 5514");
                    udplog::set_nic(&n);
                    udplog::dump(1);
                    console::status("network");
                    if cmdline.contains("wdt=probe") {
                        wdt::pet();
/// The menu key under a click at cell (col, row) on the splash, if any.
pub fn menu_hit(col: usize, row: usize) -> Option<u8> {
    if unsafe { VGA_LOG } || !splash_shown() {
        return None;
    }
    if let Some((first, n)) = unsafe { CHOOSER } {
        return (first..first + n).contains(&row).then(|| b'1' + (row - first) as u8);
    }
    if row != status_row() + 4 {
        return None;
    }
    unsafe { (*core::ptr::addr_of!(MENU_HITS)).iter().find(|&&(a, b, k)| k != 0 && (a..b).contains(&col)).map(|h| h.2) }
}

/// Key hints under the status line: each key as a light chip, then its label.
pub fn menu(items: &[(&str, &str)]) {
    if unsafe { VGA_LOG } || !splash_shown() {
        return;
    }
    let row = status_row() + 4;
    let w: usize = items.iter().map(|(k, l)| k.len() + 2 + 1 + l.len()).sum::<usize>() + 4 * items.len().saturating_sub(1);
    put(row, 0, BG, &[b' '; MAXW][..cols()]);
    let mut x = cols().saturating_sub(w) / 2;
    let hits = unsafe { &mut *core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(MENU_HITS) };
    *hits = [(0, 0, 0); 8];
    for (n, (k, l)) in items.iter().enumerate() {
        if n < 8 {
            hits[n] = (x, x + k.len() + 3 + l.len(), k.as_bytes()[0]);
        }
        put(row, x + 1, KEY, k.as_bytes());
        x += k.len() + 2;
        put(row, x + 1, TEXT, l.as_bytes());
        x += 1 + l.len() + 4;
    }
}

static mut CORNER: Option<[u8; 5]> = None; // overlay text in the top-right corner (REC), drawn over any screen

fn corner_cell(i: usize) -> Option<u16> {
    let c = unsafe { (*core::ptr::addr_of!(CORNER))? };
    let at = cols().checked_sub(5)?;
    (at..at + 5).contains(&i).then(|| 0x4000 | c[i - at] as u16) // background-dark text on red: 5.6:1 (white was 3.1:1)
}

/// A red marker in the top-right corner, an overlay like the pointer: the SSH terminal, a font change or the splash
/// cannot erase it while it is set (Codex review). None removes it and shows the real cells again.
pub fn corner(s: Option<&[u8]>) {
    unsafe {
        let mut c = [b' '; 5];
        if let Some(t) = s {
            for (d, &b) in c[1..].iter_mut().zip(t) {
                *d = b;
            }
        }
        CORNER = s.map(|_| c);
        let at = cols() - 5;
        for i in at..at + 5 {
            let v = shown(i, cell_get(i));
            if (*core::ptr::addr_of!(FB)).is_some() {
                draw(i, v, CURSOR == Some(i));
            } else {
                VGA.add(i).write_volatile(v);
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Footer bar: `left` at the left edge, `right` at the right edge.
pub fn footer(left: &[u8], right: &[u8]) {
    if unsafe { VGA_LOG } || !splash_shown() {
        return;
    }
    put(rows() - 2, 0, BG, &[b' '; MAXW][..cols()]);
    put(rows() - 2, 2, LINE, &[0xC4; MAXW][..cols().saturating_sub(4)]); // inset to the text margins
    put(rows() - 1, 0, BG, &[b' '; MAXW][..cols()]);
    put(rows() - 1, 2, dim(), left);
    let x = cols().saturating_sub(right.len() + 2);
    put(rows() - 1, x, dim(), right);
    if let Some(k) = right.iter().rposition(|&b| b == b' ') {
        put(rows() - 1, x + k + 1, TEXT, &right[k + 1..]); // the clock (last field) stands out
    }
}

/// The splash owns the screen iff its logo is there (the SSH terminal or the log may have drawn over it).
fn splash_shown() -> bool {
    title_rect().is_some_and(|(r0, c0, _, _)| cell_get(r0 * cols() + c0) == (TITLE | b'N' as u16))
}

/// Raw byte to COM1 (used to mirror the remote shell's output into the serial log).
pub fn serial_raw(b: u8) {
    serial_putc(b);
}

pub fn init() {
    unsafe {
        outb(COM1 + 1, 0x00); // no interrupts
        outb(COM1 + 3, 0x80); // DLAB
        outb(COM1 + 0, 0x01); // divisor 1 = 115200
        outb(COM1 + 1, 0x00);
        outb(COM1 + 3, 0x03); // 8N1
        outb(COM1 + 2, 0xC7); // FIFO

    pub fn glyph(&self, c: u8) -> &[u16] {
        &self.rows[c as usize * self.h..(c as usize + 1) * self.h]
    }
}

    /// Attribute Controller index 0x10 (mode control) bit 3: 1 = attribute bit 7 blinks, 0 = it selects the bright
    /// background colours 8..15. Returns (before, after). Index 0x30 keeps PAS (bit 5) set so the screen stays on.
    pub fn blink_off() -> (u8, u8) {
        unsafe {
            inb(0x3DA); // reset the index/data flip-flop
            outb(0x3C0, 0x30);
            let old = inb(0x3C1);
            outb(0x3C0, old & !0x08);
            inb(0x3DA);
            outb(0x3C0, 0x30);
            let new = inb(0x3C1);
            inb(0x3DA);
            outb(0x3C0, 0x20); // index write with PAS set (display on); the flip-flop now expects data
            (old, new)
        }
    }

    pub fn flush() {
        let t = t();
        for i in 0..t.w * t.h {
            crate::console::cell(i, t.cells[i]); // only changed cells are redrawn (VGA or framebuffer)
        }
        crate::console::cursor(if t.cursor_on { Some(t.row * t.w + t.col.min(t.w - 1)) } else { None });
    }
}

/// Take over the screen: bright backgrounds instead of blink, reset the terminal, clear the screen, home the cursor.
#[cfg(not(test))]
pub fn init() {
    let (old, new) = hw::blink_off();
    crate::kprintln!("term: VGA attribute mode control 0x{:02x} -> 0x{:02x} (bit 3 blink {})", old, new, if new & 8 == 0 { "off: bright backgrounds" } else { "STILL ON" });
    hw::t().resize(crate::console::cols(), crate::console::rows()); // the screen's grid (80x25 VGA, larger on the panel)
    hw::flush();
}

/// Replies the terminal owes the host (DSR, DA); the caller sends them on the channel.
#[cfg(not(test))]
pub fn take_reply(out: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    hw::t().take_reply(out)
}

/// Replies sent so far: DSR 6n, DSR 5n, DA1, DA2, dropped.
#[cfg(not(test))]
pub fn reply_counts() -> [u32; 5] {
    hw::t().counts
}

/// Feed one byte of the remote's output (drawn on the next `flush`).
#[cfg(not(test))]
pub fn feed(b: u8) {
    hw::t().feed(b);
}

/// Copy the grid to VGA memory and move the hardware cursor (once per batch of output, not per byte).
#[cfg(not(test))]
pub fn flush() {
    hw::flush();
}

/// DECCKM (CSI ?1h): the keyboard sends ESC O A.. for arrows instead of ESC [ A..
/// bound (the caller resets); reboot and chain-load never return.
pub fn home(ctl: &mut bmctl::Ctl, iface: &mut Interface, dev: &mut Nic, sockets: &mut SocketSet, udp_h: SocketHandle,
            tcp_h: SocketHandle, signkey: &SignKey, lport: u16) {
    kbd::init();
    console::splash();
    console::status("ready");
    console::menu(if console::is_fb() { MENU_FB } else { MENU_TEXT });
    kprintln!("home: menu up, bmctl serving");
    let (mut log_view, mut n_sess) = (false, 0u16);
    let mut chooser: Option<usize> = None; // the host chooser is open, with this row selected
    loop {
        wdt::pet();
        crate::net::rx_budget(Some(32)); // bounded ingress per poll so bmctl's deadline checks always run
        iface.poll(now(), dev, sockets);
        if let Some(a) = ctl.step(sockets.get_mut::<udp::Socket>(udp_h), time::now_ms()) {
            if !ctl.act(a, dev) {
                return;
            }
        }
        let (mut k, mut n) = ([0u8; 16], 0usize);
        if kbd::poll(&mut k, &mut n) {
            kprintln!("kbd: Ctrl-Alt-Del -> hard reset");
            wdt::hard_reset();
        }
        if let Some(c) = mouse_step(None) {
            if c == b'F' {
                if !log_view {
                    console::status(crate::hda::ptt_toggle()); // the F12 chip
                }
            } else if n < k.len() {
                k[n] = c;
                n += 1;
            }
        }
        if kbd::take_ptt() && !log_view {
            console::status(crate::hda::ptt_toggle());
        }
        let mut heard = [0u8; 76];
        let hn = crate::hda::take_typed(&mut heard[6..]);
        if hn > 0 && !log_view {
            heard[..6].copy_from_slice(b"heard ");
            console::status(core::str::from_utf8(&heard[..6 + hn]).unwrap_or("heard (not UTF-8)"));
        }
        let slots: heapless::Vec<usize, 8> = hosts().iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, h)| h.is_some()).map(|(i, _)| i).collect();
        let mut connect: Option<usize> = None; // index into `slots`
        let mut i = 0;
        while i < n {
            let b = k[i];
            i += 1;
            if let Some(sel) = chooser {
                if b == 0x1b && i + 1 < n + 1 && i < n && (k[i] == b'[' || k[i] == b'O') && i + 1 < n {
                    let dir = k[i + 1];
                    i += 2;
                    let sel = match dir {
                        b'A' => sel.saturating_sub(1),
                        b'B' => (sel + 1).min(slots.len().saturating_sub(1)),
                        _ => sel,
                    };
                    chooser = Some(sel);
                    draw_chooser(&slots, sel);
                    continue;
                }
                match b {
                    b'1'..=b'8' if ((b - b'1') as usize) < slots.len() => connect = Some((b - b'1') as usize),
                    b'\r' | b'\n' => connect = Some(sel),
                    0x1b | b'q' | b'Q' => {
                        chooser = None;
                        console::splash();
                        console::status("ready");
                        console::menu(if console::is_fb() { MENU_FB } else { MENU_TEXT });
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
                continue;
            }
            match (b | 0x20, log_view) {
                (b's', false) if slots.len() == 1 => connect = Some(0),
                (b's', false) => {
                    chooser = Some(0);
                    draw_chooser(&slots, 0);
                }
                (b'l', _) => {
                    log_view = !log_view;
                    if log_view {
                        console::show_log();
                    } else {
                        console::splash();
                        console::status("ready");
                        console::menu(if console::is_fb() { MENU_FB } else { MENU_TEXT });
                    }
                }
                (b'z', false) => {
                    if let Some((c, r)) = console::next_font() {
                        kprintln!("home: Z -> text size {}x{}", c, r);
                        console::splash();
                        console::status("text size changed (Z again for the next)");
                        console::menu(if console::is_fb() { MENU_FB } else { MENU_TEXT });
                    }
                }
                (b'r', false) => {
                    kprintln!("home: R -> restart");
                    console::status("restarting");
                    wdt::hard_reset();
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        if let Some(t) = connect.and_then(|c| slots.get(c)).and_then(|&s| hosts()[s].clone()) {
            chooser = None;
            console::splash();
            let mut st = heapless::String::<64>::new();
            let _ = core::fmt::write(&mut st, format_args!("connecting to {} ({}@{}:{})", t.name, t.user, t.ip, t.port));
            console::status(&st);
            kprintln!("home: SSH session -> {}", t.name);
            n_sess = n_sess.wrapping_add(1);
            let (idle, shown) = session(iface, dev, sockets, tcp_h, udp_h, Some(&mut *ctl), signkey, lport.wrapping_add(n_sess), &t);
            if idle {
                return;
            }
            console::splash();
            st.clear();
            let _ = core::fmt::write(&mut st, format_args!("{}: {}", t.name, if shown { "session ended" } else { "host did not answer" }));
            console::status(&st);
            console::menu(if console::is_fb() { MENU_FB } else { MENU_TEXT });
        }
    }
fn mouse_step(keys: Option<(&mut [u8], &mut usize)>) -> Option<u8> {
    let (dx, dy, wheel, btn) = kbd::take_mouse()?;
    let (x0, y0, old) = unsafe { PTR };
    let (cw, ch) = (console::cols() as i32, console::rows() as i32);
    let (x, y) = ((x0 + dx).clamp(0, cw * MX - 1), (y0 - dy).clamp(0, ch * MY - 1));
    unsafe { PTR = (x, y, btn) };
    let (col, row) = ((x / MX) as usize, (y / MY) as usize);
    let moved = (col, row) != ((x0 / MX) as usize, (y0 / MY) as usize);
    console::pointer(Some(row * cw as usize + col));
    let Some((buf, n)) = keys else {
        // act on the release, so no button is still held when a session starts
        return if btn & 1 == 0 && old & 1 != 0 { console::menu_hit(col, row) } else { None };
    };
    let (mode, sgr) = term::mouse();
    if mode == 0 {
        return None;
    }
    let mut emit = |b: u8, press: bool| {
        let mut s = heapless::Vec::<u8, 24>::new();
        if sgr {
            let mut t = heapless::String::<24>::new();
            let _ = core::fmt::write(&mut t, format_args!("\x1b[<{};{};{}{}", b, col + 1, row + 1, if press { 'M' } else { 'm' }));
            let _ = s.extend_from_slice(t.as_bytes());
        } else {
            // X10: raw bytes 32 + value; columns past 95 are bytes >= 0x80, not UTF-8 (Codex review)
            let code = if press { b } else { 3 };
            let _ = s.extend_from_slice(&[0x1b, b'[', b'M', 32 + code, 32 + 1 + col.min(222) as u8, 32 + 1 + row.min(222) as u8]);
        }
        if buf.len() - *n >= s.len() {
            buf[*n..*n + s.len()].copy_from_slice(&s);
            *n += s.len();
        }
    };
    // PS/2 bits: 0 left, 1 right, 2 middle; xterm buttons: 0 left, 1 middle, 2 right
    for (bit, b) in [(1u8, 0u8), (4, 1), (2, 2)] {
        if (btn ^ old) & bit != 0 {
            emit(b, btn & bit != 0);
        }
    }
    if moved && (mode == 1003 || (mode == 1002 && btn != 0)) {
        let held = [(1u8, 0u8), (4, 1), (2, 2)].iter().find(|(bit, _)| btn & bit != 0).map_or(3, |p| p.1);
        emit(32 + held, true);
    }
    for _ in 0..wheel.unsigned_abs().min(5) {
        emit(if wheel < 0 { 64 } else { 65 }, true); // PS/2 Z < 0 = wheel up
    }
    None
}

pub fn native_fb() -> Option<crate::console::Fb> {
    let b = pci::find(0x8086, 0xA011).or_else(|| pci::find(0x8086, 0xA001))?;
    let bar = pci::read32(b, 0x10) as u64 & !0xF;
    let gmadr = pci::read32(b, 0x18) as u64 & !0xF;
    for off in [0x61000u32, 0x61180, 0x61200, 0x70000, 0x71000, 0x71400] { // 0x61000 page also holds 0x61230
        if !nic::map_uc_4k(bar + off as u64) {
            return None;
        }
    }
    let (htot, vtot, lvds) = (rd(bar, 0x61000), rd(bar, 0x6100c), rd(bar, 0x61180));
    let (w, h) = ((htot & 0xFFF) + 1, (vtot & 0xFFF) + 1);
    if lvds & (1 << 31) == 0 || lvds & (1 << 30) == 0 || (w, h) != (1024, 600) {
        kprintln!("gma: native fb refused: LVDS {:#x}, pipe B active {}x{}", lvds, w, h);
        return None;
    }
    pci::write32(b, 4, pci::read32(b, 4) & 0xFFFF | 0x6); // memory + bus master (display fetch)
    // Uncached BEFORE the clear (review MF-D): clearing through the boot's write-back mapping could leave dirty lines that
    // a later eviction writes over pixels drawn uncached.
    if !nic::map_fb(gmadr, 4096 * 600) {
        kprintln!("gma: aperture {:#x} could not be mapped uncached: staying on VGA text", gmadr);
        return None;
    }
    // Clear the first screen of the aperture before the plane shows it.
    for i in 0..(1024 * 600) as u64 {
        unsafe { core::ptr::write_volatile((gmadr + i * 4) as *mut u32, 0) };
    }
    // Linux's i9xx order: planes off, pipe off (wait for it), then panel fitter and source size, pipe on, plane on.
    // Attempt 1 changed PIPEBSRC with the pipe running: the fitter ignored the disable and kept its 640x400 ratios,
    // and PIPEBSTAT showed a FIFO underrun (black screen).
    wr(bar, 0x71400, rd(bar, 0x71400) | (1 << 31)); // VGACNTRL: VGA plane off
    wr(bar, 0x71180, rd(bar, 0x71180) & !(1 << 31)); // plane B off
    wr(bar, 0x71184, 0);
    // gen3 PIPECONF has no pipe-state bit (it read 0x80000000 throughout attempt 2): wait two frames instead.
    let pf0 = (rd(bar, 0x61230), rd(bar, 0x61234), rd(bar, 0x61238));
    wr(bar, 0x71008, rd(bar, 0x71008) & !(1 << 31)); // PIPEBCONF: pipe off
    crate::wdt::delay_ms(40);
    wr(bar, 0x61230, 0); // PFIT_CONTROL (0x61230 on gen3/4; 0x61200 is PP_STATUS): fitter off, source = native
    wr(bar, 0x6101c, (1023 << 16) | 599); // PIPEBSRC
    wr(bar, 0x71008, rd(bar, 0x71008) | (1 << 31)); // pipe on
    crate::wdt::delay_ms(40);
    kprintln!("gma: PFIT ctl/pgm/auto {:x?} -> ctl {:#x}", pf0, rd(bar, 0x61230));
    wr(bar, 0x71188, 4096); // DSPBSTRIDE
    wr(bar, 0x71190, (599 << 16) | 1023); // DSPBSIZE
    wr(bar, 0x7118c, 0); // DSPBPOS
    // DSPBCNTR: on, 32 bpp xRGB, pipe B. Gamma (bit 30) stays OFF: the VBIOS's palette is the 8-bit VGA one, and through
    // it the 32 bpp colours came out wrong (cyan white, grey chips white; camera shot proof/cam-native-gamma-on.jpg).
    wr(bar, 0x71180, (1 << 31) | (6 << 26) | (1 << 24));
    wr(bar, 0x71184, 0); // DSPBADDR: aperture offset 0 (writing it latches the plane registers)
    wr(bar, 0x71024, rd(bar, 0x71024) | (1 << 31)); // PIPEBSTAT: clear the underrun flag (RW1C) so `gma` shows new ones
    crate::wdt::delay_ms(50);
    if rd(bar, 0x71024) & (1 << 31) != 0 {
        kprintln!("gma: FIFO underrun after the switch: back to the VBIOS VGA mode");
        restore_vga_regs(bar);
        return None;
    }
    kprintln!("gma: native 1024x600 plane B on: DSPBCNTR {:#x} PIPEBSRC {:#x} PFIT {:#x} VGACNTRL {:#x}", rd(bar, 0x71180),
        rd(bar, 0x6101c), rd(bar, 0x61230), rd(bar, 0x71400));
    Some(crate::console::Fb { addr: gmadr, pitch: 4096, w: 1024, h: 600, bytes: 4, rgb: [(16, 8), (8, 8), (0, 8)] })
struct Pt([u64; 512]);
static mut UC_PT: [Pt; 4] = [const { Pt([0; 512]) }; 4];
static mut UC_USED: usize = 0;

/// Framebuffer (multiboot video mode): mark the 2 MiB identity PDEs covering [base, base+len) uncached (PCD|PWT) so
/// pixel writes reach the screen whatever the firmware's MTRRs say. Refused (false) above 4 GiB, over available RAM,
/// or over a PDE already split into 4 KiB pages.
pub fn map_fb(base: u64, len: u64) -> bool {
    if len == 0 || base.checked_add(len).is_none_or(|e| e > 1 << 32) || crate::mb::ram_overlaps(base, len) {
        return false;
    }
    unsafe {
        for i in (base >> 21)..=((base + len - 1) >> 21) {
            let pde = addr_of_mut!(pd[i as usize]);
            let v = read_volatile(pde);
            if v & (1 << 7) == 0 {
                return false;
            }
            write_volatile(pde, v | (1 << 4) | (1 << 3));
        }
        core::arch::asm!("mov {t}, cr3", "mov cr3, {t}", t = out(reg) _, options(nostack));
        core::arch::asm!("wbinvd", options(nostack)); // no write-back line of the old mapping may land on the screen later
    }
    true
}

/// Make ONLY the 4 KiB page holding `base` uncached (PCD|PWT): split the 2 MiB identity PDE that covers it into
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""q35 check of the touchpad/mouse path (QEMU PS/2 mouse driven over QMP): the pointer moves, a left click on the
home screen's S chip opens the SSH session, and inside it the remote's ?1002/?1006 mouse tracking gets SGR reports
(press, drag, release, wheel). Needs: tools/docker-sshd/run.sh sshd_config.mouse (the remote turns tracking on, then
dumps the 24 bytes it receives with od). Screenshots to argv[1]. Exit 1 on any failure."""
import json, os, socket, subprocess, sys, time
out = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "/tmp/mouse-q35"
os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
L, sock = "/tmp/mouse-q35.log", f"/tmp/mouse-q35-{os.getpid()}.qmp"
if os.path.exists(L): os.unlink(L)
q = subprocess.Popen(["qemu-system-x86_64", "-machine", "q35", "-m", "128", "-display", "none", "-no-reboot",
    "-global", "ICH9-LPC.noreboot=false", "-serial", f"file:{L}", "-qmp", f"unix:{sock},server=on,wait=off",
    "-kernel", "target/attended/x86_64-unknown-none/release/nc110-kernel", "-initrd", "test-keys/client.key",
    "-append", "bmctl nic=mmio", "-netdev", "user,id=n0", "-device", "rtl8139,netdev=n0"])
fail = 0
def log(): return open(L, errors="replace").read() if os.path.exists(L) else ""
def wait(p, secs):
    t = time.time()
    while time.time() - t < secs:
        if p in log(): return True
        time.sleep(0.3)
    return False
def check(ok, what):
    global fail
    print(("ok   " if ok else "FAIL ") + what); fail |= not ok
time.sleep(1)
s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX); s.connect(sock); f = s.makefile("rw"); f.readline()
def qmp(c):
    f.write(json.dumps(c) + "\n"); f.flush()
    while True:
        r = json.loads(f.readline())
        if "return" in r or "error" in r: return r
qmp({"execute": "qmp_capabilities"})
def ev(*events): qmp({"execute": "input-send-event", "arguments": {"events": list(events)}}); time.sleep(0.15)
def move(dx, dy):  # small steps: PS/2 deltas are 9-bit
    while dx or dy:
        sx, sy = max(-60, min(60, dx)), max(-60, min(60, dy))
        ev({"type": "rel", "data": {"axis": "x", "value": sx}}, {"type": "rel", "data": {"axis": "y", "value": sy}})
        dx, dy = dx - sx, dy - sy
def btn(b, down): ev({"type": "btn", "data": {"down": down, "button": b}})
def shot(n): qmp({"execute": "screendump", "arguments": {"filename": f"{os.path.abspath(out)}/{n}.png", "format": "png"}})
check(wait("home: menu up", 40), "home menu up")
check("touchpad/mouse on" in log(), "PS/2 AUX mouse initialised: " + next((l.strip() for l in log().splitlines() if "touchpad/mouse" in l), "-"))
move(-400, 400)          # into the bottom-left corner (clamped)
move(81, -89)            # col 13, row 17: the S item (home layout 2026-10-21)

codex
Most is sound, including the title’s slice arithmetic. **The hard constraint holds for normal NC110 boots, but not unconditionally:** `src/gma.rs` is unchanged; adding `q35fb` on real hardware now bypasses its native initialization.

**MUST-FIX**

- **Preserve the GMA path when the emulator device is absent.** [main.rs:110](~/nc110-baremetal/src/main.rs:110):
  ```rust
  if cmdline.split(' ').any(|w| w == "q35fb") {
      if let Some(fb) = q35_fb() { ... }
  } else if native {
      if let Some(fb) = gma::native_fb() { ... }
  }
  ```
  On an NC110 with `q35fb`, the PCI lookup returns `None`, but `gma::native_fb()` is still skipped. Thus the set of display-register writes changes. Select the emulator branch only after identifying its device; otherwise retain the original native path. Without `q35fb`, the GMA calls and register-programming code remain unchanged. The new Bochs port writes themselves are gated by `pci::find(0x1234, 0x1111)?`.

- **A terminal’s white `N` can re-enable splash updates over SSH content.** [console.rs:511](~/nc110-baremetal/src/console.rs:511):
  ```rust
  cell_get(r0 * cols() + c0) == (TITLE | b'N' as u16)
  ```
  `TITLE` is ordinary bright-white-on-black (`0x0F00`). After terminal takeover, remote output placing that character at the sentinel position makes `splash_shown()` true—even with `TITLE_ON == false`. The periodic bmctl footer can then overwrite the terminal’s bottom two rows. Use explicit screen ownership for footer/status/menu gating. Normal SSH mouse events use terminal routing, so this does **not** automatically turn SSH clicks into home-menu actions.

**SHOULD**

- **Title invalidation does not invalidate already-rendered pixels.** [console.rs:160](~/nc110-baremetal/src/console.rs:160):
  ```rust
  if sh[i] == v { return; }
  ...
  TITLE_ON = false;
  ```
  A changed `0x0F00` character inside the rectangle remains rendered as title pixels. A different attribute disables the overlay globally but redraws only that cell; other slices remain until separately repainted, and identical shadow writes skip repainting entirely.

  This is a latent rendering-contract defect, **not a demonstrated normal SSH/log transition failure**: `term::init()` resets and flushes a `0x0700` blank screen, while `show_log()` calls `fill(ATTR)`, which disables the overlay first. Both clear the title successfully. Explicit ownership transitions plus repainting the rectangle would remove the ambiguity.

- **Keep VGA’s REC marker legible.** [console.rs:466](~/nc110-baremetal/src/console.rs:466):
  ```rust
  0x4000 | c[i - at] as u16
  ```
  The new framebuffer contrast rationale does not apply to VGA’s unchanged hardware palette. This changes REC from white-on-dark-red to black-on-dark-red in text mode. Use the previous `0x4F00` attribute for VGA.

- **The screenshot script can report success without reaching the requested screens.** [looks-q35-shots.py:33](~/nc110-baremetal/tools/looks-q35-shots.py:33): `wait()` silently falls through on timeout, screenshot QMP errors are ignored, and final success checks file existence. Existing PNGs can also satisfy failed captures. Require successful waits/QMP responses and fresh artifacts; verify SSH actually opened before labeling its screenshot.

**NIT / checks that passed**

- **No overlay index/slice panic found.** For the 15×3-cell rectangle:
  ```rust
  py = (relative_row * f.h + y) / 3;
  px = (relative_col * f.w + x) / 3;
  ```
  Hence `py < f.h`, `px < 5*f.w`, and `px/f.w < 5`. Both shifts stay within `0..f.w`. I exhaustively checked these indices for **12×24, 8×16, and 6×12**, and verified each font contains 256 complete glyphs. `[..w]` is at most 15 elements.

- **Narrow widths:** `cols().checked_sub(w)?` safely suppresses the title below 15 framebuffer columns. It also makes `splash_shown()` false, disabling footer/status/menu/clicks. That is a functional limitation for hypothetical narrow grids, but current framebuffer admission and supported fonts keep the grid at least 80 columns.

- **Normal menu detection is sound:** pointer highlighting leaves the shadow sentinel unchanged; status, chooser, footer, and menu rows do not overlap the title. No normal-home false negative found. The revised text-mode click target `(13,17)` matches the new layout.

- **Clear cost:** [console.rs:250](~/nc110-baremetal/src/console.rs:250) performs **614,400 volatile stores, 2.34 MiB**, per 1024×600×32 clear. `map_fb()` makes this memory uncached, so hardware timing matters. However, the diff removes the additional `use_framebuffer()` clear: this replaces two previous clears with one, rather than adding a framebuffer pass. The separate GMA clear remains unchanged. A boot `font=` option still causes another clear. No measured boot-time regression established.

- `recshow` is confined to the `q35fb` branch and uses the existing corner overlay. Apart from REC contrast, I found no functional VGA text-mode regression.

Static review and arithmetic checks only; no QEMU or hardware timing run. **7/10 — bounded rendering math and mostly sound transitions; fix ownership detection and the unconditional GMA bypass before merging.**
